\displaystyle\;\nu\int_{0}^{t}\|\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu\int_{0}^{t}\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(2-\gamma)/2},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-3/2})\tau^{-\gamma}\cdot\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\cdot\tau^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left[\nu\int_{0}^{t/2}\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu t)^{-(2-\gamma)/2},\,(\nu t)^{-3/2})\tau^{-\gamma}\,d\tau\right.

\displaystyle\;\quad\left.+\nu\int_{t/2}^{t}\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(2-\gamma)/2},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-3/2})t^{-\gamma}\,d\tau\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left[\lambda^{-1}t^{-\gamma}\min(\lambda^{-\gamma}(\nu t)^{\gamma/2},\,\lambda(\nu t)^{-1/2})+\lambda^{-1}t^{-\gamma}\min(\lambda^{-\gamma}(\nu t)^{\gamma/2},\,1)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-1}t^{-\gamma}\min(\lambda^{-2}\nu t,\,1)^{\gamma/2}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}},

where the constant
C
depends only on
\gamma
.

By Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.15
with
\varphi(t)=t^{-\gamma}
,

\displaystyle\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu t^{-\gamma}\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}(t-\tau)^{(2-\gamma)/2}\cdot\tau^{\gamma}\|\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right|^{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}(t-\tau)^{(3-\gamma)/2}\cdot\tau^{\gamma}\|\nabla\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right|^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}.

By (
2.13
) and Lemma
3.1
,

\displaystyle\|h_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq

\displaystyle\;\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+\|(\mathrm{Id}-e^{\nu t\Delta})u_{X(t)}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+C\|u_{X(t)}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-1}t^{-\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}},

and similarly,

\|h_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}.

Here the constants
C
’s only depend on
\gamma
.

To show the
L^{p}
-estimate for
u_{X}^{\nu}
with
p\in[1,\infty)
, we apply Lemma
3.1
to find that, for
\tau\in[0,t]
,

\displaystyle\;\|\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L^{p}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\|\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{\frac{1}{p}}\|\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\Big((\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{2}}^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left[\min\Big(\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2},\;(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{2}}^{2}\Big)\right]^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min\left(\lambda^{-1+\frac{1}{p}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1+\frac{1}{2p}}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{2}}^{\frac{2}{p}}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2-\frac{2}{p}},\;(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{p}}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{2}}^{2}\right).

For
p\in[1,\infty)
, we integrate the first bound above in
\tau
to obtain that

\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq\nu\int_{0}^{t}\|\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L^{p}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\,d\tau\\
\leq C(\nu t)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lambda^{-1+\frac{1}{p}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{2}}^{\frac{2}{p}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2-\frac{2}{p}},

where the constant
C
depends only on
p
.
If additionally
p\in(2,\infty)
, we use the both bounds above to similarly derive that

\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq C\lambda^{-1+\frac{2}{p}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{2}}^{\frac{4}{p}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2-\frac{4}{p}},

where
C
depends only on
p
.
The
L^{p}
-estimate for
h_{X}^{\nu}
then follows from (
2.13
) and Lemma
3.1
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
3.3
.

By (
2.13
) and Lemma
A.1
, for arbitrary
0\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\;\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t_{1})-u_{X}^{\nu}(t_{2})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\nu\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\|\Delta e^{\nu(t_{2}-\tau)\Delta}u_{X(\tau)}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\,d\tau+\left\|(e^{\nu(t_{2}-t_{1})\Delta}-\mathrm{Id})u_{X}^{\nu}(t_{1})\right\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}(\nu(t_{2}-\tau))^{-1+\gamma/2}\|u_{X(\tau)}\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\gamma}}\,d\tau+C(\nu(t_{2}-t_{1}))^{\gamma/2}\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t_{1})\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t_{2}-t_{1}))^{\gamma/2}t_{1}^{-\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t_{2}}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}.

In the last line, we applied Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
.
∎

3.3.
Estimates for
u_{X}-u_{Y}
and
u^{\nu}_{X}-u^{\nu}_{Y}

In order to bound
u_{X}-u_{Y}
and
u^{\nu}_{X}-u^{\nu}_{Y}
, we fix
t>0
, and start with the calculation

(3.20)

\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d}{d\theta}\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{\theta X+(1-\theta)Y})\,d\theta=\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{\theta X+(1-\theta)Y}[X-Y]\,d\theta.

Here by (
3.15
), with the time-variable omitted and the Einstein summation convention adopted,

(3.21)

\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{X}[Z](x)_{i}=\int_{\mathbb{T}}\partial_{s}[K_{ij}(x-X(s))]Z_{j}^{\prime}(s)-\partial_{s}[Z_{l}(s)\partial_{l}K_{ij}(x-X(s))]X_{j}^{\prime}(s)\,ds.

It satisfies the following estimate.

Lemma 3.16
.

Suppose
X\in O^{\lambda}
and
Z\in C^{1}(\mathbb{T})
.
Then for
k\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\;|\nabla^{k}\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{X}[Z]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-(2+k)/2},\,t^{-(3+k)/2})\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\min\Big(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3+k-\gamma)/2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big),

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\quad\lambda^{-1}t^{-(3+k)/2}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\Big),

where the constant
C
depends on
k
and
\gamma
.

Proof.

Let us write
\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{X}[Z]=I_{1}[t;X,Z]+I_{2}[t;X,Z]+I_{3}[t;X,Z]
, where

(3.22)

\displaystyle I_{1}[t;X,Z](x)_{i}:=\int_{\mathbb{T}}\partial_{s}[K_{ij}(x-X(s),t)]Z_{j}^{\prime}(s)\,ds,

(3.23)

\displaystyle I_{2}[t;X,Z](x)_{i}:=-\int_{\mathbb{T}}Z_{l}^{\prime}(s)\partial_{l}K_{ij}(x-X(s),t)X_{j}^{\prime}(s)\,ds,

(3.24)

\displaystyle I_{3}[t;X,Z](x)_{i}:=\int_{\mathbb{T}}Z_{l}(s)X_{m}^{\prime}(s)\partial_{m}\partial_{l}K_{ij}(x-X(s),t)X_{j}^{\prime}(s)\,ds.

In what follows, we shall omit the
t
-dependence.

For
k\in\mathbb{N}
,

\nabla^{k}I_{1}[X,Z](x)_{i}=-\int_{\mathbb{T}}X_{l}^{\prime}(s)\partial_{l}\nabla^{k}K_{ij}(x-X(s))Z_{j}^{\prime}(s)\,ds,

so Lemma
3.12
and Lemma
3.14
imply that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{k}I_{1}[X,Z](x)|\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|X^{\prime}(s)||Z^{\prime}(s)|}{(t+|x-X(s)|^{2})^{(3+k)/2}}\,ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-(2+k)/2},\,t^{-(3+k)/2})\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}.

Similarly,

|\nabla^{k}I_{2}[X,Z](x)|\leq C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-(2+k)/2},\,t^{-(3+k)/2})\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}.

As for
I_{3}
, with
s_{x}
introduced in (
2.8
),

(3.25)

\begin{split}\nabla^{k}I_{3}[X,Z](x)_{i}=&\;\int_{\mathbb{T}}X_{m}^{\prime}(s)\partial_{m}\partial_{l}\nabla^{k}K_{ij}(x-X(s))X_{j}^{\prime}(s)Z_{l}(s)\,ds\\
=&\;\int_{\mathbb{T}}X_{m}^{\prime}(s)\partial_{m}\partial_{l}\nabla^{k}K_{ij}(x-X(s))\big(X_{j}^{\prime}(s)Z_{l}(s)-X_{j}^{\prime}(s_{x})Z_{l}(s_{x})\big)\,ds.\end{split}

So we can analogously derive two estimates

|\nabla^{k}I_{3}[X,Z](x)|\leq C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|X^{\prime}(s)|^{2}|Z(s)|}{(t+|x-X(s)|^{2})^{(4+k)/2}}\,ds\leq C\lambda^{-1}t^{-(3+k)/2}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},

and (cf. (
3.18
))

(3.26)

\begin{split}|\nabla^{k}I_{3}[X,Z](x)|\leq&\;C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\otimes Z\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}|s-s_{x}|^{\gamma}}{(t+\lambda^{2}|s-s_{x}|^{2})^{(4+k)/2}}\,ds\\
\leq&\;C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3+k-\gamma)/2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big).\end{split}

Summarizing the above estimates, we obtain the desired bound.
∎

Remark 3.1
.

It is clear from (
3.26
) that this estimate also holds with
\gamma=1
.

Lemma 3.17
.

Suppose
X,Y\in O^{\lambda}
, satisfying that
\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}(\mathbb{T})}\leq\lambda/2
.
For any
t>0
and
k\in\mathbb{N}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\;\|\nabla^{k}\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-(2+k)/2},\,t^{-(3+k)/2})\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\min\Big(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3+k-\gamma)/2}\big(\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X-Y\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X-Y\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big),

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\quad\lambda^{-1}t^{-(3+k)/2}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X-Y\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\Big),

where the constant
C
depends on
k
and
\gamma
.
This estimate also holds for
\gamma=1
.

Proof.

By the assumptions,
|\theta X+(1-\theta)Y|_{*}\geq\lambda/2
for any
\theta\in[0,1]
.
Then the estimate follows from (
3.20
), Lemma

3.16
, and Remark
3.1
.
∎

This enables us to prove Lemma
3.4
.

Proof of Lemma
3.4
.

Since

(3.27)

e^{t\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})=-\int_{t}^{\infty}\Delta e^{\tau\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\,d\tau,

we apply Lemma
3.17
to find that, for arbitrary
\beta\in(0,1]
,

\displaystyle\;\big\|e^{t\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{t}^{\infty}\big\|\Delta e^{\tau\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\big\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{t}^{\infty}\lambda^{-(1-\beta)}\tau^{-1-\beta/2}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad+\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\tau^{-(3-\gamma)/2}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\big(\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X-Y\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X-Y\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-(1-\beta)}t^{-\beta/2}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(1-\gamma)/2}\big(\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X-Y\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X-Y\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big),

where the constant
C
depends on
\gamma
and
\beta
.

On the other hand, by Lemma
3.17
, for
k=0,1
,

\displaystyle\;\|\nabla^{k}\Delta e^{\tau\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-1}\tau^{-(2+k)/2}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\tau^{-(3+k-\gamma)/2}\big(\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X-Y\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X-Y\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big).

By (
3.27
) and the last estimate in Lemma
3.15
, for
t>0
,

\displaystyle\;\big\|e^{t\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\big\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\left|\sup_{\tau>t}\tau^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\|\Delta e^{\tau\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right|^{1-\gamma}\left|\sup_{\tau>t}\tau^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla\Delta e^{\tau\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right|^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-1}t^{-\gamma/2}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-1/2}\big(\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X-Y\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X-Y\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big),

where
C
depends only on
\gamma
.
∎

Next we prove Lemma
3.5
.
The following estimate will be useful.

Lemma 3.18
.

For any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
,
r>0
, and
m>1
,

\int_{B(x,r)}(t+|y-X(s)|^{2})^{-m}\,dy\leq Cr^{2}\left(1+\frac{r^{2}}{t}\right)^{m-1}\big(t+r^{2}+|x-X(s)|^{2}\big)^{-m},

where the constant
C
only depends on
m
.

Proof.

By a change of variable,

\int_{B(x,r)}(t+|y-X(s)|^{2})^{-m}\,dy=t^{-m+1}\int_{B\big(\frac{x-X(s)}{\sqrt{t}},\frac{r}{\sqrt{t}}\big)}(1+|y|^{2})^{-m}\,dy.

If
|x-X(s)|\geq 2r
, then

\displaystyle\int_{B(x,r)}(t+|y-X(s)|^{2})^{-m}\,dy\leq

\displaystyle\;Ct^{-m+1}\cdot\frac{r^{2}}{t}\left(1+\left|\frac{x-X(s)}{\sqrt{t}}\right|^{2}\right)^{-m}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}\big(t+r^{2}+|x-X(s)|^{2}\big)^{-m},

where the constant
C
depends on
m
only.
Otherwise, if
|x-X(s)|\leq 2r
, then

\displaystyle\int_{B(x,r)}(t+|y-X(s)|^{2})^{-m}\,dy\leq

\displaystyle\;t^{-m+1}\int_{B\big(0,\frac{3r}{\sqrt{t}}\big)}(1+|y|^{2})^{-m}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Ct^{-m+1}\min\left(\frac{r^{2}}{t},\,1\right)\leq Ct^{-m+1}\left(\frac{t}{r^{2}}+1\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}\left(\frac{t+r^{2}}{t}\right)^{m-1}(t+r^{2})^{-m}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}\left(1+\frac{r^{2}}{t}\right)^{m-1}\big(t+r^{2}+|x-X(s)|^{2}\big)^{-m}.

Here
C
depends on
m
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
3.5
.

Recall that
\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{X}[Z]
was defined in (
3.21
).
We first show that, if
X\in O^{\lambda}
and
Z\in C^{1}(\mathbb{T})
, for arbitrary
x\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
r>0
,

(3.28)

\begin{split}&\;\int_{B(x,r)}|\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{X}[Z](y)|\,dy\\
\leq&\;Cr^{2}\lambda^{-\gamma}t^{-(2-\gamma)/2}(t+r^{2})^{-1/2}\cdot\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}),\end{split}

where the constant
C
depends on
\gamma
.

We still split
\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{X}[Z]
as in the proof of Lemma
3.16
.
By (
3.22
), Lemma
3.12
, Lemma
3.18
, and also Lemma
3.14
,

\displaystyle\int_{B(x,r)}|I_{1}[X,Z](y)|\,dy\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}(1+r^{2}/t)^{1/2}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|X^{\prime}(s)||Z^{\prime}(s)|}{(t+r^{2}+|x-X(s)|^{2})^{3/2}}\,ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}(1+r^{2}/t)^{1/2}\cdot\min(\lambda^{-1}(t+r^{2})^{-1},(t+r^{2})^{-3/2})\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}(1+r^{2}/t)^{1/2}\cdot\lambda^{-\gamma}(t+r^{2})^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},

where
C
is a universal constant.
Similarly,

\int_{B(x,r)}|I_{2}[X,Z](y)|\,dy\leq Cr^{2}(1+r^{2}/t)^{1/2}\cdot\lambda^{-\gamma}(t+r^{2})^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}.

For
I_{3}
, by (
3.25
) and Lemma
3.13
,

\displaystyle|I_{3}[X,Z](y)|\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\otimes Z\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}|s-s_{y}|^{\gamma}}{(t+|y-X(s)|^{2})^{2}}\,ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-\gamma}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|y-X(s)|^{\gamma}}{(t+|y-X(s)|^{2})^{2}}\,ds.

Further integrating in
y
and applying Lemma
3.14
and Lemma
3.18
gives

\displaystyle\;\int_{B(x,r)}|I_{3}[X,Z](y)|\,dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}(1+r^{2}/t)^{(2-\gamma)/2}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{1}{(t+r^{2}+|x-X(s)|^{2})^{2-\gamma/2}}\,ds

\displaystyle\;\cdot\lambda^{-\gamma}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(1+r^{2}/t)^{(2-\gamma)/2}\cdot(t+r^{2})^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big),

where the constant
C
depends on
\gamma
only.
Summarizing the above estimates and using the fact that
\lambda\leq\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}
, we obtain (
3.28
).
This and (
3.20
) further imply that, if
X,Y\in O^{\lambda}
satisfy
\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\lambda/2
, for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
r>0
,

\displaystyle\|\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{X}-u_{Y})\|_{L^{1}(B(x,r))}\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{2}\lambda^{-\gamma}t^{\gamma/2-1}(t+r^{2})^{-1/2}\cdot\lambda^{-1}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\big(\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X-Y\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big),

where
C
depends only on
\gamma
.

By (
2.13
) and the assumption that
X(s,0)=Y(s,0)
,

\displaystyle\;\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t)-u_{Y}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L^{1}(B(x,r))}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\nu\int_{0}^{t}\|\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{Y(\tau)})\|_{L^{1}(B(x,r))}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu\int_{0}^{t}r^{2}\lambda^{-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu(t-\tau)+r^{2})^{-1/2}\cdot\lambda^{-1}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\big(\|(X^{\prime}(\tau),Y^{\prime}(\tau))\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X(\tau)-Y(\tau)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu r^{2}\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu(t-\tau)+r^{2})^{-1/2}\tau^{-\gamma}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|(X^{\prime}(\eta),Y^{\prime}(\eta))\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{-\mu_{0}}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\;+C\nu r^{2}\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}}\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu(t-\tau)+r^{2})^{-1/2}\,d\tau.

Since

\displaystyle\;\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu(t-\tau)+r^{2})^{-1/2}\,d\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\int_{0}^{t}(\nu\tau)^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu\tau+r^{2})^{-1/2}\,d\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\nu^{-1}r^{\gamma-1}\int_{0}^{\nu t/r^{2}}s^{\gamma/2-1}(s+1)^{-1/2}\,ds\leq C\nu^{-1}r^{\gamma-1},

and

\displaystyle\;\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu(t-\tau)+r^{2})^{-1/2}\tau^{-\gamma}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{0}^{t/2}(\nu t)^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu t+r^{2})^{-1/2}\tau^{-\gamma}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{t/2}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu(t-\tau)+r^{2})^{-1/2}t^{-\gamma}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C(\nu t)^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu t+r^{2})^{-1/2}t^{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+Ct^{-\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma/2-1}(\nu(t-\tau)+r^{2})^{-1/2}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Ct^{-\gamma}\nu^{-1}r^{\gamma-1},

we arrive at the estimate

\displaystyle\;\|u_{X}^{\nu}(t)-u_{Y}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L^{1}(B(x,r))}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Cr^{1+\gamma}\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\Big[t^{1-\gamma-\mu_{0}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|(X^{\prime}(\eta),Y^{\prime}(\eta))\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\quad+\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}}\Big],

where the constant
C
depends on
\gamma
and
\mu_{0}
.
This together with the definition of the Morrey norm in (
2.1
) implies the desired claim.
∎

3.4.
Estimates for
h_{X}^{\nu}
and
H_{X}^{\nu}

Proof of Lemma
3.6
.

Recall the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality:

(3.29)

\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\leq C\|X-Y\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}(\mathbb{T})}^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}(\mathbb{T})}^{1-\gamma}\leq C\|X-Y\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}(\mathbb{T})}^{\gamma}\|(X^{\prime},Y^{\prime})\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}(\mathbb{T})}^{1-\gamma},

where
C
depends on
\gamma
.

By Lemma
3.17
, the interpolation inequality (
3.29
), with
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma]
and
\mu_{0},\mu,\mu^{\prime}\in[0,1)
, and
k\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\;\big\|\nabla^{k}\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{X(t)})\big\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min\left(\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{2+k}{2}},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime}(\tau),X^{\prime}(t))\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}^{1-\gamma}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C\min\left(\lambda^{-1-\gamma^{\prime}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k-\gamma^{\prime}}{2}},\,\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime}(\tau),X^{\prime}(t))\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min\left(\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{2+k}{2}},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime}(\tau),X^{\prime}(t))\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}^{1-\gamma}\left(\int_{\tau}^{t}\eta^{-\mu}\cdot\eta^{\mu}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\,d\eta\right)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C\min\left(\lambda^{-1-\gamma^{\prime}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k-\gamma^{\prime}}{2}},\,\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\left(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\|(X^{\prime}(\tau),X^{\prime}(t))\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\int_{\tau}^{t}\eta^{-\mu_{0}}\cdot\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\,d\eta\right.

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\left.+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\tau}^{t}\eta^{-\mu^{\prime}}\cdot\eta^{\mu^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\,d\eta\right).

Observe that, for
\beta\in[0,1)
,

(3.30)

\begin{split}\int_{\tau}^{t}\zeta^{-\beta}\,d\zeta\leq&\;\begin{cases}(t-\tau)\tau^{-\beta},&\mbox{if }\tau\in[\frac{t}{2},t),\\
Ct^{1-\beta},&\mbox{if }\tau\in(0,\frac{t}{2}],\end{cases}\\
\leq&\;C(t-\tau)t^{-\beta},\end{split}

where
C
depends on
\beta
;
yet, we note that if
\beta\in[0,\frac{1}{2}]
, the constant
C
can be made independent of
\beta
.
Hence,

(3.31)

\begin{split}&\;\big\|\nabla^{k}\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{X(t)})\big\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\\
\leq&\;C\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{2+k}{2}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;\cdot\left(\tau^{-\gamma}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(|t-\tau|t^{-\mu}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{\gamma}\\
&\;+C\min\left(\lambda^{-1-\gamma^{\prime}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k-\gamma^{\prime}}{2}},\,\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3+k}{2}}\right)\\
&\;\quad\cdot\left(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\cdot\tau^{-\gamma^{\prime}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\cdot|t-\tau|t^{-\mu_{0}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\right.\\
&\;\qquad\quad\left.+\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\cdot|t-\tau|t^{-\mu^{\prime}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\right),\end{split}

where the constant
C
depends on
k
,
\gamma
,
\gamma^{\prime}
,
\mu_{0}
,
\mu
, and
\mu^{\prime}
.

On one hand, by (
2.14
), we directly integrate (
3.31
) to find that, for
k=0,1
,

\displaystyle\;\|\nabla^{k}h_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\nu\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{k}\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{X(t)})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)(t-\tau)^{-\frac{2+k}{2}+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\;\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{0}^{t}\min\left(\lambda^{-\gamma^{\prime}},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\right)(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1+k-\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\lambda^{-1}\left(\tau^{-\gamma^{\prime}}t^{-\mu_{0}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+t^{-\mu^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\right)d\tau,

where
C
depends on
k
,
\gamma
,
\gamma^{\prime}
,
\mu_{0}
,
\mu
, and
\mu^{\prime}
.

It remains to calculate the two integrals above.
Recall that
\gamma\in(\frac{k}{2},1)
.
If
\nu t\leq 4\lambda^{2}
, we find that

\displaystyle\;\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)(t-\tau)^{-\frac{2+k}{2}+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\lambda^{-1}(t-\tau)^{-\frac{2+k}{2}+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\lambda^{-1}t^{-\frac{k}{2}+\gamma^{2}}=C(\nu t)^{-\frac{k}{2}}\lambda^{-1}t^{-\gamma(\mu-\gamma)},

where
C
depends on
k
and
\gamma
.
Otherwise, if
\nu t>4\lambda^{2}
,

\displaystyle\;\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)(t-\tau)^{-\frac{2+k}{2}+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\left[\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t/2}t^{-\frac{3+k}{2}+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t/2}^{t-\lambda^{2}/\nu}(t-\tau)^{-\frac{3+k}{2}+\gamma}t^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau\right]

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\int_{t-\lambda^{2}/\nu}^{t}\lambda^{-1}(t-\tau)^{-\frac{2+k}{2}+\gamma}t^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-\frac{1+k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\left[t^{-\frac{1+k}{2}+\gamma^{2}}+t^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\left(t^{-\frac{1+k}{2}+\gamma}+\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\nu}\right)^{-\frac{1+k}{2}+\gamma}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}\lambda^{-1}t^{-\mu\gamma-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\nu}\right)^{-\frac{k}{2}+\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left[(\nu t)^{-\frac{1+k}{2}}+(\nu t)^{-\gamma}\lambda^{-1-k+2\gamma}\right]t^{-\gamma(\mu-\gamma)}.

Here we used the facts that
\gamma\in(\frac{k}{2},1)
and

\int_{t/2}^{t-\lambda^{2}/\nu}(t-\tau)^{-\frac{3+k}{2}+\gamma}\,d\tau\leq C\left[t^{-\frac{1+k}{2}+\gamma}+\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\nu}\right)^{-\frac{1+k}{2}+\gamma}\right].

In summary, for
k=0,1
and
\gamma\in(\frac{k}{2},1)
,

\displaystyle\;\nu^{-\frac{k}{2}}t^{-\mu\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)(t-\tau)^{-\frac{2+k}{2}+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left[(\nu t)^{-\frac{k}{2}}\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\mathds{1}_{\{\nu t\geq 4\lambda^{2}\}}(\nu t)^{-\gamma}\lambda^{-1-k+2\gamma}\right]t^{-\gamma(\mu-\gamma)}.

where the constant
C
depends on
k
and
\gamma
.
One can similarly calculate that, with arbitrary
\beta\in[0,1)
,

\displaystyle\;\int_{0}^{t}\min\left(\lambda^{-\gamma^{\prime}},\,(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\right)(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1+k-\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\tau^{-\beta}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C(\nu t)^{-\frac{1+k-\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\min\left(\lambda^{-\gamma^{\prime}},\,(\nu t)^{-\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\right)\cdot t^{1-\beta}\left[1+\mathds{1}_{\{\nu t\geq 4\lambda^{2}\}}\int_{2\lambda^{2}/(\nu t)}^{1}\zeta^{-\frac{1+k}{2}}\,d\zeta\right],

where the constant
C
depends on
k
and
\gamma^{\prime}
.
Substituting these results into the above estimate, we obtain the desired bound.

On the other hand, by (
3.31
), for
\alpha\in(0,1)\cup(1,1+\gamma^{\prime}]
satisfying that
\alpha\leq 2\gamma
, and any
k\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\;(t-\tau)^{\frac{2+k-\alpha}{2}}\big\|\nabla^{k}\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{X(t)})\big\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min\left(\lambda^{-1},(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\nu^{-\frac{2+k}{2}}(t-\tau)^{\gamma-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\tau^{-\gamma(1-\gamma)}t^{-\mu\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C\min\left(\lambda^{-1-\gamma^{\prime}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1+\gamma^{\prime}-\alpha}{2}},\,\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\right)\nu^{-\frac{4+k-\alpha}{2}}

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\left(\tau^{-\gamma^{\prime}}t^{-\mu_{0}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\left.+t^{-\mu^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\right).

Let
[\alpha]
denote the largest integer that is no greater than
\alpha
.
We shall apply Lemma
3.15
with
\varphi(\tau):=\tau^{-\gamma}
to (
2.14
).
Since
\gamma\geq\frac{\alpha}{2}
,
\gamma\geq\gamma^{\prime}
, and
\alpha-[\alpha]\in(0,1)
, we find that

\displaystyle\;\|\nabla^{[\alpha]}h_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\alpha-[\alpha]}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu t^{-\gamma}\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}(t-\tau)^{\frac{2+[\alpha]-\alpha}{2}}\tau^{\gamma}\|\nabla^{[\alpha]}\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{X(t)})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right|^{1-(\alpha-[\alpha])}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}(t-\tau)^{\frac{3+[\alpha]-\alpha}{2}}\tau^{\gamma}\|\nabla^{1+[\alpha]}\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{X(t)})\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\right|^{\alpha-[\alpha]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-\gamma}t^{-\gamma(1-\gamma+\mu)}\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}\left[\min\left(\lambda^{-1},(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)(\nu(t-\tau))^{\gamma-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\;\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-1}t^{-\gamma}\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}\left[\tau^{\gamma}\min\left(\lambda^{-1-\gamma^{\prime}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1+\gamma^{\prime}-\alpha}{2}},\,\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\right)\tau^{-\gamma^{\prime}}t^{-\mu_{0}}\right]

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-1}t^{-\gamma}\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}\left[\tau^{\gamma}\min\left(\lambda^{-1-\gamma^{\prime}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1+\gamma^{\prime}-\alpha}{2}},\,\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\right)t^{-\mu^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left[(\nu t)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\mathds{1}_{\{\nu t\geq 4\lambda^{2}\}}(\nu t)^{-\gamma}\lambda^{-1-\alpha+2\gamma}\right]t^{-\gamma(\mu-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\right)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C\left[\min\left(\lambda^{-\gamma^{\prime}},\,(\nu t)^{-\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\right)(\nu t)^{-\frac{1+\alpha-\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\lambda^{-1}+\mathds{1}_{\{\nu t\geq 4\lambda^{2}\}}(\nu t)^{-1}\lambda^{-\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\left(t^{1-\gamma^{\prime}-\mu_{0}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\left.+t^{1-\mu^{\prime}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma^{\prime}}}\right).

Here we used the fact that, since
\gamma\geq\frac{\alpha}{2}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\left[\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)(\nu\eta)^{\gamma-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\;\begin{cases}\lambda^{-1}(\nu t)^{\gamma-\frac{\alpha}{2}},&\mbox{if }\nu t\leq\lambda^{2},\\
(\nu t)^{\gamma-\frac{1+\alpha}{2}},&\mbox{if }\nu t\geq\lambda^{2}\mbox{ and }\gamma\geq\frac{1+\alpha}{2},\\
\lambda^{-1+2\gamma-\alpha},&\mbox{if }\nu t\geq\lambda^{2}\mbox{ and }\gamma\leq\frac{1+\alpha}{2},\end{cases}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C(\nu t)^{\gamma-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\min\left(\lambda^{-1},\,(\nu t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)+C\mathds{1}_{\{\nu t\geq 4\lambda^{2}\}}\lambda^{-1-\alpha+2\gamma},

and since
\alpha\in(0,1)\cup(1,1+\gamma^{\prime}]
,

\displaystyle\;\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}\left[\min\left(\lambda^{-1-\gamma^{\prime}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1+\gamma^{\prime}-\alpha}{2}},\,\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\lambda^{-\alpha}\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}\left[\min\left(\left(\frac{\nu\tau}{\lambda^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1+\gamma^{\prime}-\alpha}{2}},\,\left(\frac{\nu\tau}{\lambda^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\begin{cases}\lambda^{-\alpha}\left(\frac{\nu t}{\lambda^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1+\gamma^{\prime}-\alpha}{2}},&\mbox{if }\nu t\leq\lambda^{2},\\
\lambda^{-\alpha}\left(\frac{\nu t}{\lambda^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}},&\mbox{if }\nu t\geq\lambda^{2}\mbox{ and }\alpha\in(0,1),\\
\lambda^{-\alpha},&\mbox{if }\nu t\geq\lambda^{2}\mbox{ and }\alpha\in(1,1+\gamma^{\prime}],\end{cases}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min\left(\lambda^{-\gamma^{\prime}},\,(\nu t)^{-\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{2}}\right)(\nu t)^{\frac{1+\gamma^{\prime}-\alpha}{2}}\lambda^{-1}+C\mathds{1}_{\{\nu t\geq 4\lambda^{2}\}}\lambda^{-\alpha}.

This proves (
3.3
).

The estimates for
H_{X}^{\nu}
follows from Lemma
B.1
.
We only note that, if
\alpha\in(1,2)
,

\displaystyle\;\|H_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{1,\alpha-1}_{s}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\|\nabla h_{X}^{\nu}(X(\cdot,t),t)X^{\prime}(\cdot,t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\alpha-1}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\|\nabla h_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\alpha-1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|X^{\prime}(\cdot,t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\alpha-1}\cdot\|X^{\prime}(\cdot,t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|\nabla h_{X}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|X^{\prime}(\cdot,t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\alpha-1}_{s}}.

∎

Next we turn to bound
H_{X}^{\nu}-H_{Y}^{\nu}
.
By definition,

(3.32)

\begin{split}&\;H_{X}^{\nu}(t)-H_{Y}^{\nu}(t)\\
=&\;h_{X}^{\nu}(t)\circ X(t)-h_{Y}^{\nu}(t)\circ Y(t)\\
=&\;-\nu\int_{0}^{t}\big[\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{X(\tau)}-u_{X(t)})\big]\circ X(t)-\big[\Delta e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}(u_{Y(\tau)}-u_{Y(t)})\big]\circ Y(t)\,d\tau.\end{split}

We need to estimate the term of the form

\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{{X+Z}}-u_{X})\circ X-\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{{Y+W}}-u_{Y})\circ Y.

For that purpose, let us denote

(3.33)

J(t;X,Z)(s):=\big[\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{{X}+Z}-u_{X})\big]\circ X=\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{X+\theta Z}[Z]\circ X\,d\theta.

Then

\displaystyle\big[\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{{X+Z}}-u_{X})\big]\circ X-\big[\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{{Y+W}}-u_{Y})\big]\circ Y=

\displaystyle\;J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,W).

Lemma 3.19
.

Suppose
X,Y\in O^{\lambda}
, and
Z,W\in C^{1,\gamma}(\mathbb{T})
satisfy that
\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})},\|W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\leq\lambda/2
.
Then

\displaystyle\;\|J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,W)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}+t^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{-1}\|J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,W)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\big(\min(\lambda^{-3}t^{-1},\,\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2})+\lambda^{-3}t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}+\lambda^{-3}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\big(t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\gamma}\big)\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-2-\gamma}\big(t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{1+\gamma}\big)\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big(\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-1},\,t^{-3/2})\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}-W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big(\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\|Z-W\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z-W\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}},

where the constant
C
only depends on
\gamma
.

Proof.

We start from writing

(3.34)

J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,W)=\big(J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,Z)\big)+\big(J(t;Y,Z)-J(t;Y,W)\big).

The second term on the right-hand side can be written as

J(t;Y,Z)-J(t;Y,W)=\Delta e^{t\Delta}(u_{Y+Z}-u_{Y+W})\circ Y,

so it can be readily bounded by applying Lemma
3.16
(also see (
3.20
)).
Indeed,

\displaystyle\;\|J(t;Y,Z)-J(t;Y,W)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}+t^{1/2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{-1}\|J(t;Y,Z)-J(t;Y,W)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{0}^{1}\|\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{Y+\theta Z+(1-\theta)W}[Z-W]\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\,d\theta

\displaystyle\;+t^{1/2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{-1}\cdot\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{1}\|\nabla\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{Y+\theta Z+(1-\theta)W}[Z-W]\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\,d\theta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-1},\,t^{-3/2})\big(\|Y^{\prime}+Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}+W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)\|Z^{\prime}-W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})\big(\|Y^{\prime}+Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}+W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\big(\|Y^{\prime}+Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}+W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\|Z-W\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})\big(\|Y^{\prime}+Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}+W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)^{2}\|Z-W\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}},

where the constant
C
depends on
\gamma
.
Here we used the fact that, by assumption,
|Y+\theta Z+(1-\theta)W|_{*}\geq\lambda/2
.
Since
\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},\|W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\leq\lambda/2\leq\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}
, this further simplifies to

(3.35)

\begin{split}&\;\|J(t;Y,Z)-J(t;Y,W)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}+t^{1/2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{-1}\|J(t;Y,Z)-J(t;Y,W)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}\\
\leq&\;C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-1},\,t^{-3/2})\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}-W^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;+C\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big(\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\|Z-W\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;+C\min(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|Z-W\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}.\end{split}

To bound the first term
(J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,Z))
on the right-hand side of (
3.34
), we define
I_{m}[t;X,Z]

(m=1,2,3)
as in the proof of Lemma
3.16
(see (
3.22
)-(
3.24
)), and further denote

J_{m}(t,\theta;X,Z)(s):=I_{m}[t;X+\theta Z,Z]\circ X(s).

Then

\displaystyle J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,Z)=

\displaystyle\;\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{{X}+\theta Z}[Z]\circ X-\mathcal{D}[\Delta e^{t\Delta}u]_{Y+\theta Z}[Z]\circ Y\,d\theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\sum_{m=1}^{3}\int_{0}^{1}J_{m}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{m}(t,\theta;Y,Z)\,d\theta.

By definition, with the
t
-dependence omitted,

J_{1}(t,\theta;X,Z)(s)_{i}=-\int_{\mathbb{T}}(X_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{l}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\,ds^{\prime},

so

\displaystyle\;(J_{1}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{1}(t,\theta;Y,Z))(s)_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;-\int_{\mathbb{T}}(X_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-Y_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{l}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;-\int_{\mathbb{T}}(Y_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{l}K_{ij}(x-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))\big|_{x=Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})}^{X(s)-X(s^{\prime})}Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\;(J_{1,1}+J_{1,2})(t,\theta;X,Y,Z)(s)_{i}.

By Lemma
3.12
and Lemma
3.14
,

\displaystyle|J_{1,1}(t,\theta;X,Y,Z)(s)|\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{3/2}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\min(\lambda^{-1}t^{-1},\,t^{-3/2})\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},

where
C
is a universal constant.
Here we used the fact that
|X+\theta Z|_{*}\geq\lambda/2
.

By Lemma
3.12
,

\displaystyle\;\Big|\partial_{l}K_{ij}(x-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))\big|_{x=Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})}^{X(s)-X(s^{\prime})}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-Y(s)+Y(s^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\;\cdot\Big[\big(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2}\big)^{-2}+\big(t+|Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2}\big)^{-2}\Big],

where we have

|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-Y(s)+Y(s^{\prime})|\leq\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}|s-s^{\prime}|.

Hence,

\displaystyle\;|J_{1,2}(t,\theta;X,Y,Z)(s)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}|s-s^{\prime}|}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}+\frac{\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}|s-s^{\prime}|}{(t+|Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}.

For future use, we calculate the following general integral with
\beta\in[0,3)
by Lemma
3.14
:

(3.36)

\begin{split}&\;\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|^{\beta}}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}\\
\leq&\;C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{\lambda^{-\beta}|X(s)+\theta Z(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{\beta}}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}\\
\leq&\;C\lambda^{-\beta}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{1}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2-\beta/2}}+\frac{(\theta|Z(s)|)^{\beta}}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}\\
\leq&\;C\Big[\min(\lambda^{-1-\beta}t^{-(3-\beta)/2},\,\lambda^{-\beta}t^{-(4-\beta)/2})+\lambda^{-1-\beta}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\beta}\Big],\end{split}

where the constant
C
may depend on
\beta\in[0,3)
.
Hence, with
\beta=1
,

\displaystyle\;|J_{1,2}(t,\theta;X,Y,Z)(s)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\big(\min(\lambda^{-2}t^{-1},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})+\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}.

Here we used the inequality
\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\leq\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}
as before to find that
\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\leq C\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}
.
Therefore,

(3.37)

\begin{split}&\;\|J_{1}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{1}(t,\theta;Y,Z)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\\
\leq&\;C\big(\min(\lambda^{-2}t^{-1},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})+\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},\end{split}

where
C
is a universal constant.
Furthermore, from

\displaystyle\partial_{s}J_{1}(t,\theta;X,Z)(s)_{i}=-\int_{\mathbb{T}}(X_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))X_{m}^{\prime}(s)\partial_{l}\partial_{m}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\,ds^{\prime},

one can similarly derive that

(3.38)

\begin{split}&\;\|J_{1}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{1}(t,\theta;Y,Z)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}\\
\leq&\;C\big(\min(\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-2})+\lambda^{-2}t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},\end{split}

where
C
is also universal.
We omit the details.

Since

J_{2}(t,\theta;X,Z)(s)_{i}=-\int_{\mathbb{T}}Z_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\partial_{l}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))(X_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\,ds^{\prime}

has a similar form as
J_{1}
, the above estimates (
3.37
) and
(
3.38
) also apply to
J_{2}
.

Finally (cf. (
3.25
)),

\begin{split}&\;J_{3}(t,\theta;X,Z)(s)_{i}\\
=&\;\int_{\mathbb{T}}Z_{l}(s^{\prime})(X_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{m}\partial_{l}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))(X_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\,ds^{\prime}\\
=&\;\int_{\mathbb{T}}(X_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{m}\partial_{l}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))\\
&\;\qquad\cdot\big[Z_{l}(s^{\prime})(X_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))-Z_{l}(s)(X_{j}^{\prime}(s)+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s))\big]\,ds^{\prime}.\end{split}

Using the last formula, we derive that

\displaystyle\;(J_{3}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{3}(t,\theta;Y,Z))(s)_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\int_{\mathbb{T}}(X_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{m}\partial_{l}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\big[Z_{l}(s^{\prime})(X_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-Y_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))-Z_{l}(s)(X_{j}^{\prime}(s)-Y_{j}^{\prime}(s))\big]\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+\int_{\mathbb{T}}(X_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-Y_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{m}\partial_{l}K_{ij}(X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\big[Z_{l}(s^{\prime})(Y_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))-Z_{l}(s)(Y_{j}^{\prime}(s)+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s))\big]\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+\int_{\mathbb{T}}(Y_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{m}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\partial_{m}\partial_{l}K_{ij}(x-\theta Z(s^{\prime}))\big|_{x=Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})}^{X(s)-X(s^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\big[Z_{l}(s^{\prime})(Y_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))-Z_{l}(s)(Y_{j}^{\prime}(s)+\theta Z_{j}^{\prime}(s))\big]\,ds^{\prime}.

Proceeding as before, we find that

\displaystyle\;\|J_{3}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{3}(t,\theta;Y,Z)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\;+t^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{-1}\|J_{3}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{3}(t,\theta;Y,Z)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\cdot\frac{1}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\big[|s-s^{\prime}|\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}|s-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big]\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\cdot\frac{1}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\big[|s-s^{\prime}|\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}|s-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big]\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{\mathbb{T}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\left(\frac{|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-Y(s)+Y(s^{\prime})|}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{5/2}}+\frac{|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-Y(s)+Y(s^{\prime})|}{(t+|Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{5/2}}\right)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\big[|s-s^{\prime}|\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}|s-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big]\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}}{(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|^{2}}{t^{1/2}(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}+\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|^{2}}{t^{1/2}(t+|Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|^{1+\gamma}}{t^{1/2}(t+|X(s)-X(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}+\frac{|s-s^{\prime}|^{1+\gamma}}{t^{1/2}(t+|Y(s)-Y(s^{\prime})-\theta Z(s^{\prime})|^{2})^{2}}\,ds^{\prime}.

Here
C
is a universal constant.
By virtue of (
3.36
),

\displaystyle\;\|J_{3}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{3}(t,\theta;Y,Z)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\;+t^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{-1}\|J_{3}(t,\theta;X,Z)-J_{3}(t,\theta;Y,Z)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\big(\min(\lambda^{-2}t^{-1},\,\lambda^{-1}t^{-3/2})+\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\big(\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+\lambda^{-1-\gamma}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\gamma}\big)

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\big[\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big]

\displaystyle\;+C\big(\min(\lambda^{-3}t^{-1},\,\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2})+\lambda^{-3}t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\big)\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\big(\lambda^{-2-\gamma}t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+\lambda^{-2-\gamma}t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{1+\gamma}\big)\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\big(\min(\lambda^{-3}t^{-1},\,\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2})+\lambda^{-3}t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}+\lambda^{-3}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\big(t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\gamma}\big)\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-2-\gamma}\big(t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{1+\gamma}\big)\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}+\theta Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},

In the last inequality, we used the fact that
t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\gamma}\leq C(t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{1+\gamma})
.
Here the constant
C
may depend on
\gamma
.

Combining this with (
3.37
) and (
3.38
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\;\|J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,Z)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\;+t^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{-1}\|J(t;X,Z)-J(t;Y,Z)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\big(\min(\lambda^{-3}t^{-1},\,\lambda^{-2}t^{-3/2})+\lambda^{-3}t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}+\lambda^{-3}t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\big(t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+t^{-3/2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\gamma}\big)\|X^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-2-\gamma}\big(t^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+t^{-2}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{1+\gamma}\big)\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big(\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|Z\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}},

where
C
depends on
\gamma
.
This together with (
3.34
) and (
3.35
) implies the desired estimate.
∎

Now we can bound
H_{X}^{\nu}-H_{Y}^{\nu}
.

Proof of Lemma
3.7
.

Let
J
be defined as in (
3.33
).
Denote

(3.39)

\begin{split}&\;\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y](s)\\
:=&\;\big[J(\nu(t-\tau);X(t),X(\tau)-X(t))-J(\nu(t-\tau);Y(t),Y(\tau)-Y(t))\big](s).\end{split}

Then by (
3.32
) and (
3.33
),

(3.40)

H_{X}^{\nu}(t)-H_{Y}^{\nu}(t)=-\nu\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\,d\tau.

Under the assumptions on
X
and
Y
, we apply Lemma
3.19
to (
3.39
) to find that, for
0<\tau<t\leq T
,

(3.41)

\begin{split}&\;\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\\
&\;+(\nu(t-\tau))^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{-1}\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}\\
\leq&\;C\big[\lambda^{-3}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1}+\lambda^{-3}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-2}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\big]\\
&\;\quad\cdot(\|X^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{2}\|X^{\prime}(t)-Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)-X^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\big[(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+(\nu(t-\tau))^{-3/2}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{\gamma}\big]\\
&\;\quad\cdot\|X^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}(t)-Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\\
&\;+C\lambda^{-2-\gamma}\big[(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}+(\nu(t-\tau))^{-2}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{1+\gamma}\big]\\
&\;\quad\cdot\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\big(\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|X^{\prime}(\tau)-X^{\prime}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}(t)-Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;+C\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1}\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}(\tau)-X^{\prime}(t)-Y^{\prime}(\tau)+Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\|X(\tau)-X(t)-Y(\tau)+Y(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;\quad\cdot\big(\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|X^{\prime}(\tau)-X^{\prime}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}(\tau)-Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\gamma}}\big)\\
&\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}^{2}\|X(\tau)-X(t)-Y(\tau)+Y(t)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}},\end{split}

where the constant
C
depends on
\gamma
.
By (
3.30
), with
\mu_{0}\in[0,\frac{1}{2}]
,

(3.42)

\begin{split}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\leq&\;\int_{\tau}^{t}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\,d\eta\\
\leq&\;\int_{\tau}^{t}\zeta^{-\mu_{0}}\,d\zeta\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}X(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\leq C(t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}N_{0},\end{split}

and similarly,

\|X(\tau)-X(t)-Y(\tau)+Y(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\leq C(t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}.

Here the constants
C
’s are universal.
Moreover, with
\beta\in[\gamma,1)
,

\displaystyle\|X(\tau)-X(t)-Y(\tau)+Y(t)\|_{C_{s}^{\beta}}\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{\tau}^{t}\zeta^{-\beta}\,d\zeta\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C(t-\tau)t^{-\beta}\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\beta}},

where
C
depends on
\beta
.
This together with the previous estimate and the interpolation inequality implies that

\displaystyle\;\|X(\tau)-X(t)-Y(\tau)+Y(t)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C(t-\tau)t^{-\gamma-\mu_{0}(1-\frac{\gamma}{\beta})}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\beta}}\right),

where the constant
C
depends on
\beta
and
\gamma
.
Also by the interpolation inequality, with
\alpha\in(0,\gamma]
satisfying
(1-\gamma)(1+\alpha)+\gamma\beta=1
, we have that

(3.43)

\begin{split}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}}\leq&\;C\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1,\alpha}}^{1-\gamma}\|X(\tau)-X(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\beta}}^{\gamma}\\
\leq&\;C\left(\tau^{-\alpha}\sup_{\eta\in[\tau,t]}\eta^{\alpha}\|X^{\prime}(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\alpha}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(\int_{\tau}^{t}\zeta^{-\beta}\cdot\zeta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}X(\zeta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\beta}}\,d\zeta\right)^{\gamma}\\
\leq&\;C\left(\tau^{-\alpha}M\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(|t-\tau|\tau^{-\beta}N_{\beta}\right)^{\gamma}\\
=&\;C|t-\tau|^{\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}M^{1-\gamma}N_{\beta}^{\gamma},\end{split}

and similarly,

\displaystyle\;\|X(\tau)-X(t)-Y(\tau)+Y(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left(\tau^{-\alpha}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\alpha}\|(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\alpha}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(|t-\tau|\tau^{-\beta}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\beta}}\right)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|t-\tau|^{\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left(\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\beta}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;C|t-\tau|^{\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}D(t),

where the constants
C
’s depend on
\beta
and
\gamma
, and where
D(t)
was defined in (
3.4
).
Finally, under the assumption that
X(s,0)=Y(s,0)
, we also find that

\displaystyle\;\|X^{\prime}(t)-Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\|(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(t)\|_{C_{s}^{\alpha}}^{1-\gamma}\|(X-Y)(t)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\beta}}^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left(t^{-\alpha}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\alpha}\|(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\alpha}}\right)^{1-\gamma}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\zeta^{-\beta}\,d\zeta\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\beta}}\right)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left(\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\gamma}\|(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(\eta)\|_{C_{s}^{\gamma}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\beta}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{\beta}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;CD(t),

where
C
depends on
\beta
and
\gamma
.

Putting all these estimates into (
3.41
) gives that

\displaystyle\;\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+(\nu(t-\tau))^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}(t)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}})^{-1}\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\lambda^{-3}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1}\Big[1+(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1}\big((t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}N_{0}\big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot M^{2}\cdot D(t)\cdot|t-\tau|^{\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}M^{1-\gamma}N_{\beta}^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\Big[1+(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\gamma/2}\big((t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}N_{0}\big)^{\gamma}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot M\cdot(t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}N_{0}\cdot t^{-\gamma}D(t)

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-2-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\Big[1+(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\big((t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}N_{0}\big)^{1+\gamma}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot M\cdot\tau^{-\gamma}M\cdot D(t)\cdot(t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}N_{0}

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1}M\cdot|t-\tau|^{\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}D(t)

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}M\cdot(t-\tau)t^{-\mu_{0}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\cdot\tau^{-\gamma}M

\displaystyle\;+C\lambda^{-1-\gamma}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}M^{2}

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot(t-\tau)t^{-\gamma-\mu_{0}(1-\frac{\gamma}{\beta})}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}+D(t)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C(t-\tau)^{-1+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}D(t)\cdot\lambda^{-3}\nu^{-1}M^{3-\gamma}N_{\beta}^{\gamma}\Big[1+\nu^{-1}t^{1-2\mu_{0}}N_{0}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;+C(t-\tau)^{-(1-\gamma)/2}\tau^{-\gamma}t^{-\mu_{0}}D(t)\cdot\lambda^{-2-\gamma}\nu^{-(3-\gamma)/2}M^{2}N_{0}\Big[1+\nu^{-1}t^{1-2\mu_{0}}N_{0}^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}

\displaystyle\;+C(t-\tau)^{-1+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}D(t)\cdot\lambda^{-1}\nu^{-1}M

\displaystyle\;+C(t-\tau)^{-(1-\gamma)/2}\tau^{-\gamma}t^{-\mu_{0}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\cdot\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\nu^{-(3-\gamma)/2}M^{2}

\displaystyle\;+C(t-\tau)^{-(1-\gamma)/2}t^{-\gamma-\mu_{0}(1-\frac{\gamma}{\beta})}\left(D(t)+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t)}\eta^{\mu_{0}}\|\partial_{t}(X-Y)(\eta)\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\right)\cdot\lambda^{-1-\gamma}\nu^{-(3-\gamma)/2}M^{2},

where
C
depends on
\beta
and
\gamma
.
By assumption
t\leq 1
, so

(3.44)

\begin{split}&\;\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\\
&\;+(\nu(t-\tau))^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L_{s}^{\infty}})^{-1}\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}}\\
\leq&\;C(t-\tau)^{-1+\gamma}\tau^{-\gamma}A(t),\end{split}

where
A(t)
was defined in (
3.5
), and where
C
depends on
\beta
and
\gamma
.

Now by (
3.40
),

\|H_{X}^{\nu}(t)-H_{Y}^{\nu}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}}\leq\nu\int_{0}^{t}\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}}\,d\tau\leq C\nu A(t).

Applying Lemma
3.15
and (
3.44
), we also find for
\gamma\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
that

\displaystyle\;\|H_{X}^{\nu}(t)-H_{Y}^{\nu}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{2\gamma}_{s}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu t^{-\gamma}\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}(t-\tau)^{1-\gamma}\tau^{\gamma}\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\right|^{1-2\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}(t-\tau)^{3/2-\gamma}\tau^{\gamma}\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}\right|^{2\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Ct^{-\gamma}\nu^{1-\gamma}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}})^{2\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\sup_{\tau\in(0,t)}(t-\tau)^{1-\gamma}\tau^{\gamma}\Big[\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{L_{s}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(\nu(t-\tau))^{1/2}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}})^{-1}\|\mathcal{J}[\tau,t,\nu;X,Y]\|_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1}(\mathbb{T})}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;Ct^{-\gamma}\nu^{1-\gamma}(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{s}})^{2\gamma}A(t).

Here the constants
C
’s depend on
\beta
and
\gamma
.
∎

3.5.
Estimates for
B_{\nu}[u]

Recall that
B_{\nu}[u]
was defined in (
2.15
).

Proof of Lemma
3.8
.

We rewrite
B_{\nu}[u]
as

B_{\nu}[u](t)=-\int_{0}^{t}e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Since
\mathbb{P}=\mathrm{Id}+\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathrm{div}\,
, the standard parabolic estimates give that,
for

q\in(1,\infty]
and
r\in[1,q]
,

\|e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L^{q}_{x}}\leq C(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{r}+\frac{1}{q}}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L^{r}_{x}}.

Here we interpret
\frac{1}{\infty}
as
0
, and the constant
C
depends on
q
and
r
.
Hence, if additionally
0<\frac{1}{r}<\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{q}
,

\displaystyle\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{L^{q}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{r}+\frac{1}{q}}(\nu\tau)^{\frac{1}{r}-1}\,d\tau\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1-\frac{1}{r}}\|(u\otimes u)(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{r}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1-\frac{1}{r}}\|(u\otimes u)(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{r}},

which gives (
3.6
).

By Lemma
3.15
with
\varphi(\tau)=\tau^{-(1+\gamma)/2}
,

\displaystyle\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\gamma}}\leq

\displaystyle\;Ct^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\left|\sup_{\tau\in[0,t]}(t-\tau)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\tau^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\right|^{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left|\sup_{\tau\in[0,t]}(t-\tau)^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}\tau^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|\nabla e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\right|^{\gamma}.

Then by Lemma
A.1
,

(3.45)

\begin{split}&\;\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\gamma}}\\
\leq&\;Ct^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t]}(t-\tau)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\cdot(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(2-\gamma)/2}\tau^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}\right|^{1-\gamma}\\
&\;\cdot\left|\sup_{\tau\in(0,t]}(t-\tau)^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}\cdot(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(3-\gamma)/2}\tau^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}\right|^{\gamma}\\
\leq&\;C\nu^{-1}t^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\sup_{\tau\in(0,t]}\tau^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}\\
\leq&\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\sup_{\tau\in(0,t]}(\nu\tau)^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L_{x}^{2/(1-\gamma)}},\end{split}

where
C
depends on
\gamma
.
This proves (
3.10
).
Based on this, for
p\in[2,\infty)
, if
\gamma\geq 1-\frac{4}{p}
, i.e.
\frac{4}{1-\gamma}\geq p
, we may use the interpolation inequality to further obtain that

(3.46)

\begin{split}&\;\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{\dot{C}_{x}^{\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\\
\leq&\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\right)^{\frac{(1-\gamma)p}{2}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)^{2-\frac{(1-\gamma)p}{2}}\\
\leq&\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1/2}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)^{2}.\end{split}

The general case
\gamma\in(0,1)
follows by interpolating this with (
3.9
), which gives (
3.11
).

To show the higher-order estimates in (
3.12
), we observe from (
2.15
) that

\displaystyle B_{\nu}[u](t)=

\displaystyle\;-\int_{t/2}^{t}e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\,d\tau+e^{\frac{\nu t}{2}\Delta}\left[-\int_{0}^{t/2}e^{\nu(t/2-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}(u\cdot\nabla u)(\tau)\,d\tau\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;-\int_{t/2}^{t}e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\,d\tau+e^{\frac{\nu t}{2}\Delta}\big(B_{\nu}[u](t/2)\big).

Applying Lemma
3.15
with
\varphi(t)=1
to the first term, we find that

\displaystyle\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{\dot{C}^{1,\gamma}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left|\sup_{\tau\in(t/2,t)}(t-\tau)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\right|^{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left|\sup_{\tau\in(t/2,t)}(t-\tau)^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\right|^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+\big\|e^{\frac{\nu t}{2}\Delta}\big(B_{\nu}[u](t/2)\big)\big\|_{\dot{C}^{1,\gamma}_{x}}.

By Lemma
A.1
and (
3.9
), for
p\in(2,\infty)
,

\displaystyle\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{\dot{C}^{1,\gamma}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle\;C\left|\sup_{\tau\in(t/2,t)}(t-\tau)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}\right|^{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left|\sup_{\tau\in(t/2,t)}(t-\tau)^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}\right|^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\;+C(\nu t)^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\big\|B_{\nu}[u](t/2)\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}\sup_{\tau\in(t/2,t)}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-(2+\gamma)/2}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1/2}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-(2+\gamma)/2}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1/2}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left[\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|u(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}\right].

This bounds the first term on the left-hand side of (
3.12
).
Similarly,

\displaystyle\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{\dot{C}^{1}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{t/2}^{t}\big\|\nabla e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\,d\tau+\big\|e^{\frac{\nu t}{2}\Delta}\big(B_{\nu}[u](t/2)\big)\big\|_{\dot{C}^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{t/2}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}\,d\tau+C(\nu t)^{-1/2}\big\|B_{\nu}[u](t/2)\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{\gamma/2}\sup_{\tau\in(t/2,t)}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-1}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1/2}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-1}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1/2}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\;\cdot\left[\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|u(\eta)\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}\right].

This bounds the second term on the left-hand side of (
3.12
).
This completes the proof of (
3.12
) for
p\in(2,\infty)
.
The case of
p=2
then follows from the interpolation inequality and the Young’s inequality.

By Lemma
A.1
,

\displaystyle\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{0}^{t}\left\|e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\right\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-1/2}\big\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\big\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}\,d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-\gamma/2}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\eta)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}},

and in a similar spirit,

\begin{split}\|B_{\nu}[u](t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\;\int_{0}^{t}\big\|e^{\nu(t-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\,d\tau\\
\leq&\;C\int_{0}^{t}(\nu(t-\tau))^{-(2-\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}\,d\tau\\
\leq&\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t)^{-1/2}\sup_{\tau\in(0,t]}(\nu\tau)^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}.\end{split}

In view of these inequalities as well as

\|u_{1}\otimes u_{2}\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq C\|u_{1}\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})},

we find that

\displaystyle\;(\nu t)^{\gamma/2}\|(B_{\nu}[u]-B_{\nu}[v])(t)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+(\nu t)^{1/2}\|(B_{\nu}[u]-B_{\nu}[v])(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{(1+\gamma)/2}\big\|(u\otimes u-v\otimes v)(\eta)\big\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{\gamma/2}\|(u-v)(\eta)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t]}(\nu\eta)^{1/2}\|(u(\eta),v(\eta))\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}},

where
C
depends on
\gamma
.
This proves (
3.13
).
One can justify (
3.14
) in a similar manner using the second last line of (
3.45
).
∎

Finally, we prove Lemma
3.9
on the time-continuity of
B_{\nu}[u]
.

Proof of Lemma
3.9
.

If
t_{1}\leq t_{2}/2
, by (
3.9
) in Lemma
3.8
,

\displaystyle\;\|B_{\nu}[u](t_{2})-B_{\nu}[u](t_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\|B_{\nu}[u](t_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|B_{\nu}[u](t_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu t_{1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{1}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{1}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-1}(\nu t_{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu(t_{2}-t_{1}))^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}(\nu t_{1})^{-\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{1}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{1}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-1}(\nu(t_{2}-t_{1}))^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}(\nu t_{2})^{-\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{p}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\right)^{2}.

If
t_{1}\in(t_{2}/2,t_{2})
, by definition,

B_{\nu}[u](t_{2})-B_{\nu}[u](t_{1})=-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{\nu(t_{2}-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\,d\tau+\big(e^{\nu(t_{2}-t_{1})\Delta}-\mathrm{Id}\big)B_{\nu}[u](t_{1}).

By Lemma
A.1
(also see (
3.45
)) and (
3.11
) in Lemma
3.8
,
and also using the assumption
t_{1}\geq t_{2}/2
,

\displaystyle\;\|B_{\nu}[u](t_{2})-B_{\nu}[u](t_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\|e^{\nu(t_{2}-\tau)\Delta}\mathbb{P}\mathrm{div}\,(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\,d\tau+\big\|\big(e^{\nu(t_{2}-t_{1})\Delta}-\mathrm{Id}\big)B_{\nu}[u](t_{1})\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}(\nu(t_{2}-\tau))^{-(2-\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\tau)\|_{M^{1,1-\gamma}}\,d\tau+C(\nu(t_{2}-t_{1}))^{\gamma/2}\|B_{\nu}[u](t_{1})\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}(\nu(t_{2}-\tau))^{-(2-\gamma)/2}(\nu\tau)^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\,d\tau\cdot\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}(\nu\eta)^{(1+\gamma)/2}\|(u\otimes u)(\eta)\|_{L^{2/(1-\gamma)}_{x}}

\displaystyle\;+C\nu^{-1}(\nu(t_{2}-t_{1}))^{\gamma/2}(\nu t_{1})^{-(1+\gamma)/2}

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{1}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{1}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)^{2}.

When
\gamma\geq 1-\frac{4}{p}
, which means
\frac{4}{1-\gamma}\geq p
, we use interpolation inequality as in (
3.46
) to find that

\displaystyle\;\|B_{\nu}[u](t_{2})-B_{\nu}[u](t_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\nu^{-1}(\nu(t_{2}-t_{1}))^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}(\nu t_{2})^{-\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}\left(\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}+\sup_{\eta\in(0,t_{2}]}(\nu\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u(\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)^{2}.

Observe that lowering the value of
\gamma
makes the bound larger, so this holds true for any
\gamma\in(0,1)
.

Combining these two cases, we prove the desired estimate.
∎

4.
Construction of the Local Solution

In this section, we shall apply a fixed-point argument to construct a local mild solution to (
1.7
)-(
1.11
).
The main result of this section is Proposition
4.1
.

4.1.
Setup of the fixed-point argument

Throughout this section, we consider the initial data
X_{0}=X_{0}(s)\in C^{1}(\mathbb{T})
, and
