\leq&\;C\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)^{2}\Big[\lambda^{-2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-3}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big].\end{split}

The Hölder estimates for
g_{X}-g_{Y}
can be justified as in the proof of Lemma
3.10
.
We first consider the case
\gamma\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
.
Given distinct
s_{1},s_{2}\in\mathbb{T}
, we only consider the case
r:=|s_{1}-s_{2}|_{\mathbb{T}}\in(0,\frac{\pi}{4})
and
s_{2}=s_{1}+r
.
Using the notations in (
C.8
), we write

(g_{X}-g_{Y})(s_{1})-(g_{X}-g_{Y})(s_{2})=\sum_{m=1}^{7}g_{m}[X]-g_{m}[Y].

We note that
g_{7}[X]=g_{7}[Y]=0
.

By (
C.3
) and (
C.18
),

\displaystyle\;|g_{1}[X]-g_{1}[Y]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{I}\frac{1}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|}\left|\nabla G\big(L_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))-\nabla G\big(L_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\right|

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot|X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})|\big|X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-X^{\prime}(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+\int_{I}\frac{1}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|}\left|\nabla G\big(L_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\right||(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(s^{\prime})||X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-X^{\prime}(s_{1})|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+\int_{I}\frac{1}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|}\left|\nabla G\big(L_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\right||Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime})|\big|(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(s^{\prime})-(X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime})(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{s_{1}-r}^{s_{1}+2r}\frac{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|}\Big[\lambda^{-2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-3}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{s_{1}-r}^{s_{1}+2r}\frac{1}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|}\cdot\lambda^{-2}|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\cdot\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{s_{1}-r}^{s_{1}+2r}\frac{1}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|}\cdot\lambda^{-2}|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\cdot\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big].

Obviously,
|g_{2}[X]-g_{2}[Y]|
satisfies the same bound.

Using (
C.9
) and (
C.18
), we derive analogously that

\displaystyle\;|g_{3}[X]-g_{3}[Y]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{|s_{1}-s_{2}|}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\Big[\lambda^{-2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-3}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{|s_{1}-s_{2}|}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{|s_{1}-s_{2}|}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big].

To bound
|g_{4}[X]-g_{4}[Y]|
, we first follow the argument in the proof of (
C.18
) to find that (also see (
C.10
)), for any
s^{\prime}\in I^{c}
and
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,

(C.20)

\begin{split}&\;\big|\nabla^{k}G\big(L_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{k}G\big(L_{X}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{k}G\big(L_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)+\nabla^{k}G\big(L_{Y}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)\big|\\
\leq&\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}||s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-(k+1)}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big].\end{split}

We omit its proof.
Using this and also (
C.10
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\;|g_{4}[X]-g_{4}[Y]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{|s_{1}-s_{2}|}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|}|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{1}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s_{2}||s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}\cdot\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{1}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s_{2}||s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}\cdot\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big].

By (
C.11
) as well as (
C.3
) and (
C.18
),

\displaystyle\;|g_{5}[X]-g_{5}[Y]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\int_{I}\frac{1}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\gamma}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\;\quad\cdot\int_{I}\frac{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|}\Big[\lambda^{-2}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-3}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big]\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\gamma}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\int_{I}\frac{1}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big].

Finally,

|g_{6}[X]-g_{6}[Y]|\leq C|s_{1}-s_{2}|\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Summarizing all the above estimates yields that, for
|s_{1}-s_{2}|\leq\frac{\pi}{4}
,

\displaystyle\;|(g_{X}-g_{Y})(s_{1})-(g_{X}-g_{Y})(s_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big].

This, together with (
C.19
), implies the desired estimate in the case
\gamma\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
.

Next we study the case
\gamma\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
.
As in (
C.12
), for
s\neq s^{\prime}
, we denote

\tilde{L}_{X}(s,s^{\prime}):=\frac{X(s^{\prime})-X(s)}{2\sin\frac{s^{\prime}-s}{2}},\quad\tilde{L}_{Y}(s,s^{\prime}):=\frac{Y(s^{\prime})-Y(s)}{2\sin\frac{s^{\prime}-s}{2}}.

Here
s^{\prime}-s
is again understood as the usual subtraction on
\mathbb{R}
.

For distinct
s_{1},s_{2}\in\mathbb{T}
, define
r
and
I
as before.
We still assume
s_{2}=s_{1}+r
and
r\in(0,\frac{\pi}{4})
without loss of generality.
Thanks to (
C.13
) and using the notations in (
C.14
),

(g_{X}-g_{Y})^{\prime}(s_{1})-(g_{X}-g_{Y})^{\prime}(s_{2})=\sum_{m=1}^{6}\tilde{g}_{m}[X]-\tilde{g}_{m}[Y].

As in (
C.18
) and (
C.20
), we also have for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
that

(C.21)

\begin{split}&\;\big|\nabla^{k}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s,s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{k}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))-\nabla^{k}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s,s^{\prime})\big)+\nabla^{k}G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\big|\\
\leq&\;C|s-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-(k+1)}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big],\end{split}

and for any
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
and
s^{\prime}\in I^{c}
,

(C.22)

\begin{split}&\;\big|\nabla^{k}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{k}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{k}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)+\nabla^{k}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)\big|\\
\leq&\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}||s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-(k+1)}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big].\end{split}

whose proofs will be omitted as they are the same as those of (
C.18
) and (
C.20
).

As a result of (
C.21
),

\displaystyle\;\Big|X_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-X_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\partial_{kl}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))

\displaystyle\;-Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)+Y_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\partial_{kl}G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\Big|\big(X_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})-Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\big)\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\big(X_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-Y_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\big)\partial_{kl}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\Big|

\displaystyle\;+\Big|Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\big[\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)\big]-Y_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\big[\partial_{kl}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))-\partial_{kl}G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\big]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\big|(X-Y)^{\prime}(s_{1})-(X-Y)^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\big|\big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)\big|

\displaystyle\;+\big|(X-Y)^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\big|\big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\big|

\displaystyle\;+\big|Y^{\prime}(s_{1})-Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\big|\big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)\big|

\displaystyle\;+|Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime})|\Big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))+\nabla^{2}G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big|\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big|^{-2}+C\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot\lambda^{-3}\big|\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})-X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\big|

\displaystyle\;+C|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\cdot\lambda^{-3}\big|\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})-\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big|

\displaystyle\;+C\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big].

Using this estimate as well as (
C.15
), we derive that

\displaystyle\;\big|\tilde{g}_{1}[X]-\tilde{g}_{1}[Y]\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\int_{I}\frac{|X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime})|}{4\sin^{2}\frac{s_{1}-s^{\prime}}{2}}\big|X_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-X_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\partial_{kl}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\big|\big|X_{j}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-X_{j}^{\prime}(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+\int_{I}\frac{|Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime})|}{4\sin^{2}\frac{s_{1}-s^{\prime}}{2}}\big|X_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-X_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\partial_{kl}G(X^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad-Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)+Y_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\partial_{kl}G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\big|\big|X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-X^{\prime}(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+\int_{I}\frac{|Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime})|}{4\sin^{2}\frac{s_{1}-s^{\prime}}{2}}\big|Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{1})\partial_{kl}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-Y_{l}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\partial_{kl}G(Y^{\prime}(s^{\prime}))\big|\big|(X-Y)^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-(X-Y)^{\prime}(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{I}\frac{\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\lambda^{-3}\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I}\frac{\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big]\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I}\frac{\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\lambda^{-3}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|(X-Y)^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)^{2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big].

|\tilde{g}_{2}[X]-\tilde{g}_{2}[Y]|
enjoys the same bound.
Similarly,

\displaystyle\;\big|\tilde{g}_{3}[X]-\tilde{g}_{3}[Y]\big|+\big|\tilde{g}_{4}[X]-\tilde{g}_{4}[Y]\big|+\big|\tilde{g}_{6}[X]-\tilde{g}_{6}[Y]\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)^{2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big].

We omit the details.
For
\tilde{g}_{5}[X]-\tilde{g}_{5}[Y]
, we first derive that

\displaystyle\;\big|\tilde{g}_{5}[X]-\tilde{g}_{5}[Y]\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{|X_{k}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})X_{l}^{\prime}(s_{2})-Y_{k}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{2})|}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)\big|\big|X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-X^{\prime}(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{|Y_{k}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{2})|}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)+\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)\big|\big|X^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-X^{\prime}(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{|Y_{k}^{\prime}(s^{\prime})Y_{l}^{\prime}(s_{2})|}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)\big|

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\big|(X-Y)^{\prime}(s^{\prime})-(X-Y)^{\prime}(s_{1})\big|\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{1}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\lambda^{-3}\big|\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})-\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big|\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\big|\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{X}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad-\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\big)+\nabla^{2}G\big(\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big)\big|\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\lambda^{-3}\big|\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{1},s^{\prime})-\tilde{L}_{Y}(s_{2},s^{\prime})\big|\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}.

By virtue of (
C.16
) and (
C.22
),

\displaystyle\;\big|\tilde{g}_{5}[X]-\tilde{g}_{5}[Y]\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{1}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\cdot\lambda^{-3}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s_{2}||s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot|s_{1}-s_{2}||s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\quad\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big]\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\;+C\int_{I^{c}}\frac{\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}}{|s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\lambda^{-3}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}|s_{1}-s_{2}||s_{2}-s^{\prime}|^{-1+\gamma}\cdot|s^{\prime}-s_{1}|^{\gamma}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\,ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)^{2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\Big].

Combining all the estimates, we obtain that

\displaystyle\;\big|(g_{X}-g_{Y})^{\prime}(s_{1})-(g_{X}-g_{Y})^{\prime}(s_{2})\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;C|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{2\gamma-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)^{2}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)

\displaystyle\;\cdot\lambda^{-3}\Big[\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\lambda^{-1}\big(\|X^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\big)\|X^{\prime}-Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big],

which implies the desired Hölder estimate in the case
\gamma\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
.
